\usepackage[fleqn]{amsmath}
\setlength{\mathindent}{1em}
\usepackage{amssymb, amsthm}
\usepackage[utf8]{inputenc}
\usepackage[margin=1in]{geometry}
\usepackage{graphicx}
\usepackage{enumitem}
\theoremstyle{plain}
\newtheorem{proposition}{Proposition}
\newtheorem*{proposition*}{Proposition}
\newcommand{\fit}[1]{\resizebox{\ifdim\width>\linewidth\linewidth\else\width\fi}{!}{#1}}

\begin{document}
\begin{proposition*}[nsl-initiator-agreement]
Suppose $n \text{ s.t. } \operatorname{is\text{-}nsl\text{-}space}(n)$. Let $c \text{ be s.t. } \operatorname{is\text{-}bundle}(c)$. If $\operatorname{bspace}(c) = \operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))$, then for every $s \in \operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, if $\langle 3, s \rangle \in \operatorname{bnodes}(c)$ then for every $p \text{ s.t. } \neg (\operatorname{kinv}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}tokens}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, for every $x \text{ s.t. } \neg (\operatorname{kinv}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}tokens}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, for every $y \text{ s.t. } \operatorname{uniquely\text{-}originates}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), y)$, for every $z \text{ s.t. } \operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(s), p, x, y, z)$, if $\neg (y = z)$ then 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists t \in \operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))). \\ \quad (\operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(t), p, x, y, z) \wedge (\langle 2, t \rangle \in \operatorname{bnodes}(c))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

\end{proposition*}

\begin{proof}
{\small\emph{The numbers in the margin are step numbers of the full proof (\texttt{proof-tex}); a range such as 13--15 is several steps shown as one row.  A claim is proved by the indented rows under it.}}\par\smallskip
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{12}] \textbf{Claim 1} (proved in steps 13--15): $\operatorname{is\text{-}pk\text{-}space}(n)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{13--15}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-nsl-space} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}nsl\text{-}space}(n)$ of the proposition, we get
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}pk\text{-}space}(n) \wedge \\ \forall i \in \operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))). \\ \quad i \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)) \vee \\ \quad \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(i), a, b, m, r) \vee \\ \quad \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(i), a, b, m, r) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 1)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{16}] By the proposition \emph{pk-space-parts} at $n := n$ (its hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}pk\text{-}space}(n)$ is in context):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}infiltrated\text{-}space}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)) \wedge \\ \operatorname{is\text{-}strand\text{-}space}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{names}(n) \in \mathbf{Set} \wedge \\ \operatorname{pubkey}(n) \in (\operatorname{names}(n) \to \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \forall u \in \operatorname{names}(n).\; (u \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \forall u \in \operatorname{names}(n).\; \neg (u \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \forall u, v \in \operatorname{names}(n). \\ \quad (\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(u) = \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(v)) \Rightarrow (u = v) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{17--23}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{is\text{-}infiltrated\text{-}space}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)) \\
\operatorname{is\text{-}strand\text{-}space}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
\operatorname{names}(n) \in \mathbf{Set} \\
\operatorname{pubkey}(n) \in (\operatorname{names}(n) \to \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
\forall u \in \operatorname{names}(n).\; (u \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \forall u, v \in \operatorname{names}(n). \\ \quad (\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(u) = \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(v)) \Rightarrow (u = v) \end{array} \\
\forall u \in \operatorname{names}(n).\; \neg (u \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
By the proposition \emph{strand-space-parts} at $s := \operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))$:
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}token\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \in \mathbf{Set} \wedge \\ \operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \in \mathbf{Set} \wedge \\ \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \in \mathbf{Set} \wedge \\ \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\ \in\; (\operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \to \operatorname{strands}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{24--28}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{is\text{-}token\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \in \mathbf{Set} \\
\operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \in \mathbf{Set} \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\ \in\; (\operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \to \operatorname{strands}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))) \end{array} \\
\operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \in \mathbf{Set}
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
\textbf{Claim 2} (proved in steps 29--31):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \forall i \in \operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))). \\ \quad i \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)) \vee \\ \quad \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(i), a, b, m, r) \vee \\ \quad \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(i), a, b, m, r) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{29--31}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-nsl-space} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}nsl\text{-}space}(n)$ of the proposition, we get
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}pk\text{-}space}(n) \wedge \\ \forall i \in \operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))). \\ \quad i \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)) \vee \\ \quad \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(i), a, b, m, r) \vee \\ \quad \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(i), a, b, m, r) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 2)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{32}] \textbf{Claim 3} (proved in steps 33--34):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} p \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ x \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ y \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ z \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \neg (y \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \wedge \\ \neg (y \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \neg (z \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(s) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, p \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(z, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \rangle \rangle \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{33--34}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-init-strand} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(s), p, x, y, z)$ of the proposition, we get
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} p \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ x \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ y \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ z \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \neg (y \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \wedge \\ \neg (y \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \neg (z \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(s) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, p \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(z, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \rangle \rangle \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 3)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{35--42}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
p \in \operatorname{names}(n) \\
x \in \operatorname{names}(n) \\
y \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
z \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
\neg (y \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \\
\neg (y \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(s) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, p \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(z, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \rangle \rangle \end{array} \\
\neg (z \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
Clearly $p \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$.
  \item[\textbf{43}] Instantiate the hypothesis: $\neg (p \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$
  \item[\textbf{44--45}] Clearly $\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$ and $x \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$.
  \item[\textbf{46}] Instantiate the hypothesis: $\neg (x \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$
  \item[\textbf{47--49}] Clearly
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x) \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
y \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
p \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{50}] \textbf{Claim 4} (proved in steps 51--53):
\begin{equation*}
\langle y, p \rangle \in \operatorname{tuples}(\operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))))
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{51--53}] By the axiom \emph{ti}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 4)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{54--57}] Clearly
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, p \rangle) \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, p \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \\ \in\; \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \end{array} \\
z \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
x \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{58}] \textbf{Claim 5} (proved in steps 59--62):
\begin{equation*}
\langle y, z, x \rangle \in \operatorname{tuples}(\operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))))
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{59--62}] By the axiom \emph{ti}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 5)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{63--65}] Clearly
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \\ \in\; \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \end{array} \\
\operatorname{encr}(z, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{66}] By the proposition \emph{init-strand-nodes} at $n := n$, $a := s$, $p := p$, $x := x$, $y := y$, $z := z$ (its hypotheses $\operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(s), p, x, y, z)$, $s \in \operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$ and $\operatorname{is\text{-}pk\text{-}space}(n)$ are in context):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \langle 1, s \rangle \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{is\text{-}sent}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 1, s \rangle) \wedge \\ \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 1, s \rangle) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, p \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \wedge \\ \langle 2, s \rangle \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{is\text{-}received}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 2, s \rangle) \wedge \\ \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 2, s \rangle) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \wedge \\ \langle 3, s \rangle \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{is\text{-}sent}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 3, s \rangle) \wedge \\ \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 3, s \rangle) = \operatorname{encr}(z, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{67--75}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\langle 1, s \rangle \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
\operatorname{is\text{-}sent}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 1, s \rangle) \\
\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 1, s \rangle) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, p \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \\
\langle 2, s \rangle \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
\operatorname{is\text{-}received}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 2, s \rangle) \\
\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 2, s \rangle) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \\
\langle 3, s \rangle \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 3, s \rangle) = \operatorname{encr}(z, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)) \\
\operatorname{is\text{-}sent}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 3, s \rangle)
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
\textbf{Claim 6} (proved in steps 76--78):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \forall z \in \{d \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) : \neg (d \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))\}. \\ \quad z \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{76--78}] Suppose $z \in \{d \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) : \neg (d \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))\}$. By the axiom \emph{sep-me}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 6)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{79}] Clearly $\operatorname{is\text{-}strand\text{-}space}(\operatorname{bspace}(c))$.
  \item[\textbf{80}] \textbf{Claim 7} (proved in steps 81--90): $\operatorname{same\text{-}strand\text{-}before}(\operatorname{bspace}(c), \langle 2, s \rangle, \langle 3, s \rangle)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{81}] Substituting $\operatorname{bspace}(c) = \operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))$, reduce to $\operatorname{same\text{-}strand\text{-}before}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 2, s \rangle, \langle 3, s \rangle)$
  \item[\textbf{82}] By the definition \emph{same-strand-before}, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \langle 2, s \rangle \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \langle 3, s \rangle \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle 2, s \rangle) = \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle 3, s \rangle) \wedge \\ \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle 2, s \rangle) < \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle 3, s \rangle) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{83--90}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 7)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{91--93}] Clearly
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \forall \operatorname{ssx\_}. \\ \quad \operatorname{same\text{-}strand\text{-}before}(\operatorname{bspace}(c), \operatorname{ssx\_}, \langle 3, s \rangle) \Rightarrow (\operatorname{ssx\_} \in \operatorname{bnodes}(c)) \end{array} \\
\langle 2, s \rangle \in \operatorname{bnodes}(c) \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}subterm}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \\ \quad \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)), \\ \quad \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p))) \end{array}
\end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{94}] \textbf{Claim 8} (proved in steps 95--96):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}subterm}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \\ \quad \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)), \\ \quad \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 2, s \rangle)) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{95--96}] Substituting $\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \langle 2, s \rangle) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p))$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}subterm}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \\ \quad \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)), \\ \quad \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 8)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{97}] \textbf{Claim 9} (proved in steps 98--100):
\begin{equation*}

\textbf{Claim 27} (proved in steps 203--210):
\begin{equation*}
\forall \operatorname{sat\_} \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}tokens}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)).\; (\operatorname{sat\_} \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{203--210}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-infiltrated-space} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}infiltrated\text{-}space}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))$ (from step 17), we get
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{length}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)) = 3 \wedge \\ \operatorname{is\text{-}strand\text{-}space}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)) \in \mathbf{Set} \wedge \\ \operatorname{adv\text{-}tokens}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)) \in \mathbf{Set} \wedge \\ \forall \operatorname{sap\_} \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)).\; (\operatorname{sap\_} \in \operatorname{ids}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \forall \operatorname{sat\_} \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}tokens}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)).\; (\operatorname{sat\_} \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \forall \operatorname{sap\_} \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)). \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}adversary\text{-}trace}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \operatorname{adv\text{-}tokens}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{sap\_})) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 27)}
  \end{itemize}

\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))

\textbf{Claim 28} (proved in steps 221--222): $\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{lst}(l)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{221--222}] Substituting $\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$, reduce to $\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \operatorname{lst}(l)$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 28)}
  \end{itemize}

\textbf{Claim 29} (proved in steps 232--233): $\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{encr}(h, k)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{232--233}] Substituting $\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$, reduce to $\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \operatorname{encr}(h, k)$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 29)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{234}] By the proposition \emph{encr-injective} at $g := \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle)$, $y := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)$, $u := h$, $z := k$ (its 4 hypotheses are in context): $(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = h) \wedge (\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) = k)$
  \item[\textbf{235--236}] Hence $\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) = k$ and $\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = h$. \textbf{Claim 30} (proved in steps 237--238): $\operatorname{same\text{-}strand\text{-}before}(\operatorname{bspace}(c), u_{2}, m)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{237--238}] Substituting $\operatorname{bspace}(c) = \operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))$, reduce to $\operatorname{same\text{-}strand\text{-}before}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), u_{2}, m)$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 30)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{239}] By the proposition \emph{bundle-ssb-closed} at $c := c$, $y := m$ (its 5 hypotheses are in context):
\begin{equation*}
\forall \operatorname{ssx\_}.\; (\operatorname{same\text{-}strand\text{-}before}(\operatorname{bspace}(c), \operatorname{ssx\_}, m) \Rightarrow (\operatorname{ssx\_} \in \operatorname{bnodes}(c)))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{240--241}] Clearly $u_{2} \in \operatorname{bnodes}(c)$ and $\operatorname{is\text{-}subterm}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), y, y)$.
  \item[\textbf{242}] \textbf{Claim 31} (proved in steps 243--244):

By the proposition \emph{encr-ne-list} at $g := \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, p \rangle)$, $y := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(x)$, $l := \langle y, z, x \rangle$:

\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{283--284}] Hence $\neg \operatorname{is\text{-}adversary\text{-}node}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n), m)$. By the axiom \emph{sep-me}.

  \item[\textbf{288--291}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}

\\ \quad \quad \quad \quad \quad \exists u. \\ \quad \quad \quad \quad \quad \quad (\operatorname{same\text{-}strand\text{-}before}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), u, m) \wedge m) \wedge (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), u) = h)) \end{array} \\

\\ \quad \quad \quad \quad \quad \exists u. \\ \quad \quad \quad \quad \quad \quad (\operatorname{same\text{-}strand\text{-}before}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), u, m) \wedge m) \wedge (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), u) = h)) \end{array} \\

Clearly $\operatorname{nsw\_} \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$.
  \item[\textbf{292}] \textbf{Claim 41} (proved in steps 293--296):
\begin{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{293--296}] By the axiom \emph{ti}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 41)}

  \item[\textbf{297}] By the proposition \emph{list-in-msgs} at $t := \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $k := \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $l := \langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle$ (its 17 hypotheses are in context):
\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle) \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{298}] Clearly $\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$.
  \item[\textbf{299}] \textbf{Claim 42} (proved in steps 300--302):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}subterm}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \\ \quad \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)), \\ \quad \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle)) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{300}] Substituting $\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = h$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}subterm}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \\ \quad \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)), \\ \quad h) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{301--302}] Substituting $h = \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), u_{2})$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}subterm}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \\ \quad \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)), \\ \quad \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), u_{2})) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 42)}

  \item[\textbf{303}] By the proposition \emph{subterm-of-flat-list} at $t := \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $k := \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $d := \{d \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) : \neg (d \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))\}$, $w := \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p))$, $l := \langle y, z, x \rangle$ (its 20 hypotheses are in context):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) \vee \\ \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \\ \in\; \{d \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) : \neg (d \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))\} \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{304--305}] Hence $\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle)$. By the proposition \emph{encr-ne-list} at $g := \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle)$, $y := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)$, $l := \langle y, z, x \rangle$:
\begin{equation*}
\neg (\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{306--307}] Hence $\neg \operatorname{is\text{-}adversary\text{-}node}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n), m)$. By the axiom \emph{sep-me}.

  \item[\textbf{310}] By the proposition \emph{token-not-encr} at $t := \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $g := \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle)$, $y := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)$ (its hypotheses $\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) \in \mathbf{Set}$, $\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle) \in \mathbf{Set}$ and $\operatorname{is\text{-}token\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$ are in context):
\begin{equation*}
\neg (\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, \operatorname{nsw\_}, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{311--312}] Hence $\neg \operatorname{is\text{-}adversary\text{-}node}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n), m)$. \textbf{Claim 43} (proved in steps 313--318): $\operatorname{is\text{-}regular\text{-}node}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n), m)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{313}] By the definition \emph{is-regular-node}, reduce to $\neg (\operatorname{nth}(2, m) \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$
  \item[\textbf{314--315}] Suppose $\operatorname{nth}(2, m) \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))$. \textbf{Claim 44} (proved in steps 316--317): $\operatorname{is\text{-}adversary\text{-}node}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n), m)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{316--317}] By the definition \emph{is-adversary-node}, reduce to $\operatorname{nth}(2, m) \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 44)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{318--319}] By the proposition \emph{nsl-regular-encr-subterm} at $n := n$, $x := m$, $g := \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle)$, $y := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)$ (its 6 hypotheses are in context):
\begin{equation*}

  \emph{(this proves Claim 43)}

  \item[\textbf{321--322}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-regular-node} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}regular\text{-}node}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n), m)$ (from step 317), we get $\neg (\operatorname{nth}(2, m) \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 45)}
  \end{itemize}

\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{324--331}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{nth}(2, m) \in \operatorname{adv\text{-}ids}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)) \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r) \vee \\ \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r) \end{array} \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r) \end{array} \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists b, m, r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r) \end{array} \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists m, r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r) \end{array} \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r) \end{array} \\
\operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r)

\textbf{Claim 46} (proved in steps 332--333):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} a \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ b \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ m \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ r \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \neg (m \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \wedge \\ \neg (m \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \neg (r \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle \rangle \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{332--333}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-init-strand} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r)$ (from step 330), we get
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} a \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ b \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ m \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ r \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \neg (m \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \wedge \\ \neg (m \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \neg (r \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle \rangle \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 46)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{334--341}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
a \in \operatorname{names}(n) \\
\operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r) \\
b \in \operatorname{names}(n) \\
m \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
r \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
\neg (m \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \\
\neg (m \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle \rangle \end{array} \\
\neg (r \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
Clearly $a \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$.
  \item[\textbf{342}] Instantiate the hypothesis: $\neg (a \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$
  \item[\textbf{343--344}] Clearly $\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a) \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$ and $b \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$.
  \item[\textbf{345}] Instantiate the hypothesis: $\neg (b \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$
  \item[\textbf{346--348}] Clearly
\begin{equation*}

m \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
a \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}

  \item[\textbf{349}] \textbf{Claim 47} (proved in steps 350--352):
\begin{equation*}
\langle m, a \rangle \in \operatorname{tuples}(\operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))))
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{350--352}] By the axiom \emph{ti}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 47)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{353}] By the proposition \emph{list-in-msgs} at $t := \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $k := \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $l := \langle m, a \rangle$ (its hypothesis $\operatorname{pubkey}(n) \in (\operatorname{names}(n) \to \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$ is in context):
\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle) \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{354--356}] Clearly
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \\ \in\; \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \end{array} \\
r \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
b \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{357}] \textbf{Claim 48} (proved in steps 358--361):
\begin{equation*}
\langle m, r, b \rangle \in \operatorname{tuples}(\operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))))
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{358--361}] By the axiom \emph{ti}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 48)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{362}] By the proposition \emph{list-in-msgs} at $t := \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $k := \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $l := \langle m, r, b \rangle$ (its hypothesis $\operatorname{pubkey}(n) \in (\operatorname{names}(n) \to \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$ is in context):
\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle) \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{363--364}] Clearly
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \\ \in\; \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \end{array} \\
\operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{365}] By the proposition \emph{three-strand-node} at $s := \operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))$, $n := m$, $d := \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle$, $x := \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \rangle$, $y := \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle$ (its 2 hypotheses are in context):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 1 \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \vee \\ \operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 2 \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \rangle)) \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \rangle)) \vee \\ \operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 3 \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{366--369}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 1 \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
\textbf{Claim 49} (proved in steps 370--372):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \mathit{sent}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{370--372}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 49)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{373}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{374}] \textbf{Claim 50} (proved in steps 375--377):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{375--377}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 50)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{378}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{379}] \textbf{Claim 51} (proved in steps 380--382):
\begin{equation*}

\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]

\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{381--382}] Substituting $\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 51)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{383}] By the proposition \emph{encr-injective} at $g := \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle)$, $y := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)$, $u := \operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle)$, $z := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)$ (its hypotheses $m \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$ and $\operatorname{pubkey}(n) \in (\operatorname{names}(n) \to \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$ are in context):
\begin{equation*}
((\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = \operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle)) \wedge (\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) = \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{384--385}] Hence $\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) = \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)$ and $\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = \operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle)$. By the proposition \emph{lst-injective} at $l := \langle y, z, x \rangle$, $x := \langle m, a \rangle$: $\langle y, z, x \rangle = \langle m, a \rangle$
  \item[\textbf{386}] \textbf{Claim 52} (proved in steps 387--388): $(\operatorname{length}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{length}(\langle m, a \rangle))$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{387--388}] Substituting $\langle y, z, x \rangle = \langle m, a \rangle$, reduce to $(\operatorname{length}(\langle m, a \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{length}(\langle m, a \rangle))$  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 52)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{389}] \textbf{Claim 53} (proved in steps 390--392): $\operatorname{length}(\langle m, a \rangle) = 3$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{390}] Substituting $\operatorname{length}(\langle m, a \rangle) = \operatorname{length}(\langle y, z, x \rangle)$, reduce to $\operatorname{length}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = 3$
  \item[\textbf{391--392}] By the axiom \emph{len-r}.  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 53)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{393}] \textbf{Claim 54} (proved in steps 394--395): $\operatorname{length}(\langle m, a \rangle) = 2$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]

  \end{itemize}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{397--399}] Substituting $\operatorname{length}(\langle m, a \rangle) = 2$, reduce to $(2 = 3) \Rightarrow (2 = 3)$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 55)}
  \end{itemize}

\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \forall n \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), d_{1}, d_{2}, d_{3}. \\ \quad (\operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, n)) = \langle d_{1}, d_{2}, d_{3} \rangle) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{nth}(1, n) = 1 \wedge \\ \quad \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), n) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, d_{1})) \wedge \\ \quad \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), n) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, d_{1})) \vee \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{nth}(1, n) = 2 \wedge \\ \quad \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), n) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, d_{2})) \wedge \\ \quad \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), n) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, d_{2})) \vee \\ \quad \quad \operatorname{nth}(1, n) = 3 \wedge \\ \quad \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), n) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, d_{3})) \wedge \\ \quad \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), n) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, d_{3})) \end{array} \\

\textbf{Claim 57} (proved in steps 410--412):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \rangle)) \Rightarrow \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \mathit{received}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{410--412}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 57)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{413}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{414}] \textbf{Claim 58} (proved in steps 415--417):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \rangle)) \Rightarrow \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{415--417}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 58)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{418}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{419}] \textbf{Claim 59} (proved in steps 420--421): $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{sent}$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{420--421}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-sent} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}sent}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$ (from step 160), we get $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{sent}$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 59)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{422}] \textbf{Claim 60} (proved in steps 423--425): $\mathit{sent} = \mathit{received}$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{423}] Substituting $\mathit{sent} = \operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$, reduce to $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{received}$
  \item[\textbf{424--425}] Substituting $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{received}$, reduce to $\mathit{received} = \mathit{received}$  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 60)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{426}] By the proposition \emph{sent-ne-received} (its hypotheses $m \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$ and $\operatorname{pubkey}(n) \in (\operatorname{names}(n) \to \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$ are in context): $\neg (\mathit{sent} = \mathit{received})$
  \item[\textbf{427--430}] Hence

\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{pubkey}(n) \in (\operatorname{names}(n) \to \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))) \Rightarrow  \\ (b \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \Rightarrow (\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b) \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \end{array} \\

\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, a \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle m, r, b \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(a)) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle \rangle \end{array} \\

b \in \operatorname{names}(n) \\
a \in \operatorname{names}(n) \\
\operatorname{is\text{-}init\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r) \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle))

\textbf{Claim 61} (proved in steps 431--433):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \mathit{sent}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{431--433}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 61)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{434}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{435}] \textbf{Claim 62} (proved in steps 436--438):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) \rangle)) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{436--438}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 62)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{439}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{440}] \textbf{Claim 63} (proved in steps 441--443):
\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{441}] Substituting $\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{442--443}] Substituting $\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)) = \operatorname{encr}(r, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 63)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{444}] By the proposition \emph{encr-injective} at $g := \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle)$, $y := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)$, $u := r$, $z := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)$ (its hypotheses $m \in \operatorname{nodes}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$ and $\operatorname{pubkey}(n) \in (\operatorname{names}(n) \to \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$ are in context):
\begin{equation*}
(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = r) \wedge (\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) = \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{445--446}] Hence $\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) = \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(b)$ and $\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = r$. \textbf{Claim 64} (proved in steps 447--448): $r = \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{447--448}] Substituting $\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = r$, reduce to $r = r$  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 64)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{449}] By the axiom \emph{membership-implies-sethood} at $a := \langle y, z, x \rangle$, $b := \operatorname{tuples}(\operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))))$ (its 3 hypotheses are in context): $\langle y, z, x \rangle \in \mathbf{Set}$
  \item[\textbf{450}] By the proposition \emph{token-ne-list} at $t := \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$, $x := r$, $l := \langle y, z, x \rangle$ (its hypotheses $\langle y, z, x \rangle \in \mathbf{Set}$, $r \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$ and $\operatorname{is\text{-}token\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$ are in context): $\neg (r = \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle))$
  \item[\textbf{451--456}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists a, b, m, r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), a, b, m, r) \end{array} \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists b, m, r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), \operatorname{nla\_2}, b, m, r) \end{array} \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists m, r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), \operatorname{nla\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2}, m, r) \end{array} \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \exists r. \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), \operatorname{nla\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2}, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, r) \end{array} \\
\operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), \operatorname{nla\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2}, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2})
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
\textbf{Claim 65} (proved in steps 457--458):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{nla\_2} \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ \operatorname{nlb\_2} \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ \operatorname{nlm\_2} \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \neg (\operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \wedge \\ \neg (\operatorname{nlm\_2} \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \neg (\operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle \rangle \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{457--458}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-resp-strand} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), \operatorname{nla\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2}, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2})$ (from step 455), we get
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{nla\_2} \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ \operatorname{nlb\_2} \in \operatorname{names}(n) \wedge \\ \operatorname{nlm\_2} \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \wedge \\ \neg (\operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \wedge \\ \neg (\operatorname{nlm\_2} \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \neg (\operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \wedge \\ \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle \rangle \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 65)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{459--466}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{nla\_2} \in \operatorname{names}(n) \\
\operatorname{nlb\_2} \in \operatorname{names}(n) \\
\operatorname{nlm\_2} \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
\operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))) \\
\neg (\operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{names}(n)) \\
\neg (\operatorname{nlm\_2} \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)) \\ =\; \langle \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle \rangle \end{array} \\
\neg (\operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
Clearly $\operatorname{nla\_2} \in \operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))$.
  \item[\textbf{467}] Instantiate the hypothesis: $\neg (\operatorname{nla\_2} \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$

  \item[\textbf{470}] Instantiate the hypothesis: $\neg (\operatorname{nlb\_2} \in \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))$

\operatorname{nla\_2} \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{474}] \textbf{Claim 66} (proved in steps 475--477):

\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \\ \in\; \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \end{array} \\
\operatorname{nln\_2} \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))) \\
\operatorname{nlb\_2} \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))

  \item[\textbf{482}] \textbf{Claim 67} (proved in steps 483--486):

\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \in \operatorname{msg\text{-}set}(\operatorname{tokens}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))), \operatorname{keys}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))))
