  \item[\textbf{490}] By the proposition \emph{three-strand-node} at $s := \operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n))$, $n := m$, $d := \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle$, $x := \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle$, $y := \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle$ (its 2 hypotheses are in context):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 1 \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \vee \\ \operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 2 \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle)) \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle)) \vee \\ \operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 3 \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{491--494}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 1 \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
\textbf{Claim 68} (proved in steps 495--497):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \Rightarrow \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \mathit{received}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{495--497}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 68)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{498}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{499}] \textbf{Claim 69} (proved in steps 500--502):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \Rightarrow \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nla\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2}))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{500--502}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 69)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{503}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{504}] \textbf{Claim 70} (proved in steps 505--506): $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{sent}$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{505--506}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-sent} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}sent}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$ (from step 160), we get $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{sent}$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 70)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{507}] \textbf{Claim 71} (proved in steps 508--510): $\mathit{sent} = \mathit{received}$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{508}] Substituting $\mathit{sent} = \operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$, reduce to $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{received}$
  \item[\textbf{509--510}] Substituting $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{received}$, reduce to $\mathit{received} = \mathit{received}$  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 71)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{511}] By the proposition \emph{sent-ne-received}: $\neg (\mathit{sent} = \mathit{received})$
  \item[\textbf{512--516}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 2 \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle)) \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle)) \vee \\ \operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 3 \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \wedge \\ (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \end{array} \\
\operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 2 \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle)) \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
\textbf{Claim 72} (proved in steps 517--519):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle)) \Rightarrow \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \mathit{sent}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{517--519}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 72)}

  \item[\textbf{520}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{521}] \textbf{Claim 73} (proved in steps 522--524):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{sent}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \rangle)) \Rightarrow \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2}))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]

  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{525}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{526}] \textbf{Claim 74} (proved in steps 527--529):
\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2}))
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{527}] Substituting $\operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)) = \operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2}))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{528--529}] Substituting $\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2}))$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \\ =\; \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle), \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 74)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{530}] By the proposition \emph{encr-injective} at $g := \operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle)$, $y := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p)$, $u := \operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle)$, $z := \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})$:
\begin{equation*}
((\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = \operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle)) \wedge (\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) = \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})))
\end{equation*}

  \item[\textbf{531--532}] Hence $\operatorname{pubkey}(n)(p) = \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nla\_2})$ and $\operatorname{lst}(\langle y, z, x \rangle) = \operatorname{lst}(\langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle)$. By the proposition \emph{lst-injective} at $l := \langle y, z, x \rangle$, $x := \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle$: $\langle y, z, x \rangle = \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle$
  \item[\textbf{533}] \textbf{Claim 75} (proved in steps 534--535): $(\operatorname{nth}(1, \langle y, z, x \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle))$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{534--535}] Substituting $\langle y, z, x \rangle = \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
(\operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle))
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 75)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{536}] \textbf{Claim 76} (proved in steps 537--539):
\begin{equation*}
(\operatorname{nth}(1, \langle y, z, x \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle)) \Rightarrow (y \simeq \operatorname{nlm\_2})
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{537--539}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 76)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{540}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{541}] \textbf{Claim 77} (proved in steps 542--543): $y = \operatorname{nlm\_2}$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{542--543}] Substituting $y = \operatorname{nlm\_2}$, reduce to $\operatorname{nlm\_2} = \operatorname{nlm\_2}$  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 77)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{544}] \textbf{Claim 78} (proved in steps 545--546): $(\operatorname{nth}(2, \langle y, z, x \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle))$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{545--546}] Substituting $\langle y, z, x \rangle = \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
(\operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle))
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 78)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{547}] \textbf{Claim 79} (proved in steps 548--550):
\begin{equation*}
(\operatorname{nth}(2, \langle y, z, x \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle)) \Rightarrow (z \simeq \operatorname{nln\_2})
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{548--550}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 79)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{551}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{552}] \textbf{Claim 80} (proved in steps 553--554): $z = \operatorname{nln\_2}$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{553--554}] Substituting $z = \operatorname{nln\_2}$, reduce to $\operatorname{nln\_2} = \operatorname{nln\_2}$  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 80)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{555}] \textbf{Claim 81} (proved in steps 556--557): $(\operatorname{nth}(3, \langle y, z, x \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(3, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle))$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{556--557}] Substituting $\langle y, z, x \rangle = \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
(\operatorname{nth}(3, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(3, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle))
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 81)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{558}] \textbf{Claim 82} (proved in steps 559--561):
\begin{equation*}
(\operatorname{nth}(3, \langle y, z, x \rangle) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(3, \langle \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2} \rangle)) \Rightarrow (x \simeq \operatorname{nlb\_2})
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{559--561}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 82)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{562}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{563}] \textbf{Claim 83} (proved in steps 564--565): $x = \operatorname{nlb\_2}$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{564--565}] Substituting $x = \operatorname{nlb\_2}$, reduce to $\operatorname{nlb\_2} = \operatorname{nlb\_2}$  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 83)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{566--567}] Instantiate the hypothesis: $p = \operatorname{nla\_2}$
  \item[\textbf{568}] \textbf{Claim 84} (proved in steps 569--571):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), \operatorname{nla\_2}, \operatorname{nlb\_2}, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, \operatorname{nlb\_2}, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{569--571}] Substituting $\operatorname{nla\_2} = p$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, \operatorname{nlb\_2}, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, \operatorname{nlb\_2}, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 84)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{572}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{573}] \textbf{Claim 85} (proved in steps 574--576):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, \operatorname{nlb\_2}, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{574--576}] Substituting $\operatorname{nlb\_2} = x$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 85)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{577}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{578}] \textbf{Claim 86} (proved in steps 579--581):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, \operatorname{nlm\_2}, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, y, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{579--581}] Substituting $\operatorname{nlm\_2} = y$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, y, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, y, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 86)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{582}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{583}] \textbf{Claim 87} (proved in steps 584--586):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, y, \operatorname{nln\_2}) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, y, z) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{584--586}] Substituting $\operatorname{nln\_2} = z$, reduce to
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, y, z) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad \operatorname{is\text{-}resp\text{-}strand}(n, \operatorname{strand\text{-}map}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)))(\operatorname{nth}(2, m)), p, x, y, z) \end{array}
\end{equation*}
  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 87)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{587}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{588}] \textbf{Claim 88} (proved in steps 589--591): $(m = \langle \operatorname{nth}(1, m), \operatorname{nth}(2, m) \rangle) \Rightarrow (m = \langle 2, \operatorname{nth}(2, m) \rangle)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{589--591}] Substituting $\operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 2$, reduce to $(m = \langle 2, \operatorname{nth}(2, m) \rangle) \Rightarrow (m = \langle 2, \operatorname{nth}(2, m) \rangle)$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 88)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{592}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{593}] \textbf{Claim 89} (proved in steps 594--595): $\langle 2, \operatorname{nth}(2, m) \rangle \in \operatorname{bnodes}(c)$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{594--595}] Substituting $\langle 2, \operatorname{nth}(2, m) \rangle = m$, reduce to $m \in \operatorname{bnodes}(c)$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 89)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{596--601}] Take $t :=$ $\operatorname{nth}(2, m)$  \emph{(the side condition holds by assumption)}
  \item[\textbf{602--604}] Hence 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}}
\operatorname{nth}(1, m) = 3 \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \\
(\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle))
\end{array}
\end{equation*}
\textbf{Claim 90} (proved in steps 605--607):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(1, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \mathit{received}) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{605--607}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 90)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{608}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{609}] \textbf{Claim 91} (proved in steps 610--612):
\begin{equation*}
\begin{array}{@{}l@{}} (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{nth}(2, \langle \mathit{received}, \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2})) \rangle)) \Rightarrow  \\ \quad (\operatorname{node\text{-}content}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) \simeq \operatorname{encr}(\operatorname{nln\_2}, \operatorname{pubkey}(n)(\operatorname{nlb\_2}))) \end{array}
\end{equation*}

  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{610--612}] By the axiom \emph{nth-r}.  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 91)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{613}] By the axiom \emph{detach!}.
  \item[\textbf{614}] \textbf{Claim 92} (proved in steps 615--616): $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{sent}$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{615--616}] Unfolding the definition \emph{is-sent} in the hypothesis $\operatorname{is\text{-}sent}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$ (from step 160), we get $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{sent}$  \emph{(it holds by assumption; this proves Claim 92)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{617}] \textbf{Claim 93} (proved in steps 618--620): $\mathit{sent} = \mathit{received}$
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2.8em, itemsep=4pt, parsep=1pt]
  \item[\textbf{618}] Substituting $\mathit{sent} = \operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m)$, reduce to $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{received}$
  \item[\textbf{619--620}] Substituting $\operatorname{node\text{-}dir}(\operatorname{ispace}(\operatorname{pk\text{-}inf}(n)), m) = \mathit{received}$, reduce to $\mathit{received} = \mathit{received}$  \emph{(established by reflexivity; this proves Claim 93)}
  \end{itemize}
  \item[\textbf{621}] By the proposition \emph{sent-ne-received}: $\neg (\mathit{sent} = \mathit{received})$
  \item[\textbf{622}]   \emph{(this proves Claim 22)}
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}
\end{proof}
